- **KDP A+ Content**: Use when planning or drafting the copy and module layout for an Amazon KDP book's A+ Content (the image-and-text modules in the "From the Publisher" section of the detail page). Covers what A+ Content is for, module planning, the still-images-only and no-search-index facts, the strict quote/endorsement limit, and the full list of prohibited content. Don't use for the description, keywords, categories, or title — those have their own skills. ([ArtekFitt/ai-skills](https://github.com/ArtekFitt/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/amazon-kdp/kdp-aplus-content) · ★1)
- **KDP advertising**: Use when planning, setting up, or improving an Amazon Ads campaign for a KDP book — eligibility and prerequisites, choosing Sponsored Products vs Sponsored Brands, calculating budget and bids from price/royalty (break-even and target ACOS), drafting ad copy/headlines, structuring campaigns and targeting, reading the KDP vs Ads reports, moderation, and billing troubleshooting. The agent researches, structures, and drafts; a human places bids, sets budgets, and launches. Don't use for the on-page listing (description, keywords, categories, title, A+ Content) — those have their own KDP skills. ([ArtekFitt/ai-skills](https://github.com/ArtekFitt/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/amazon-kdp/kdp-advertising) · ★1)
- **KDP categories**: Use when choosing or revising the browse categories an Amazon KDP book is shelved under. Covers the current 3-categories-per-format rule, how to pick specific, honest, winnable shelves, the category/subcategory/placement path, and the accuracy rules Amazon enforces. Don't use for backend keywords, the description, or the title — those have their own skills. ([ArtekFitt/ai-skills](https://github.com/ArtekFitt/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/amazon-kdp/kdp-categories) · ★1)
- **KDP description**: Use when writing or revising an Amazon KDP book description (the "blurb" on a book's Amazon detail page) for a fiction or nonfiction title. Covers the hook, plot-then-feeling structure, natural keyword use, comp titles, author credibility, Amazon's supported HTML, the 4,000-character limit, and the content that Amazon prohibits in descriptions. Do NOT use for back-cover copy, ad copy, or the separate Editorial Reviews section. ([ArtekFitt/ai-skills](https://github.com/ArtekFitt/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/amazon-kdp/kdp-description) · ★1)
- **KDP keywords**: Use when choosing or revising the 7 backend search keywords for an Amazon KDP book. Covers what keywords do, how to research them the way readers search, which of the 7 slots to spend on what, the phrasing rules (no quotes, space-separated words), and the full list of keyword types Amazon prohibits. Don't use for on-page copy (title, subtitle, description) or for browse categories — those have their own skills. ([ArtekFitt/ai-skills](https://github.com/ArtekFitt/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/amazon-kdp/kdp-keywords) · ★1)
- **KDP publishing**: Read first for any Amazon KDP listing or detail-page copy task — book description, backend keywords, browse categories, title/subtitle, or A+ Content. Holds the house rules every KDP task shares: use the client's project context, match their brand voice, keep metadata accurate, and stay inside Amazon's content rules. Points to the specialised KDP skill for each field. Don't use for manuscript writing, cover design, pricing, or advertising campaigns.
